# Review pack: kisumu-fiscal-ledger

Generated by `node scripts/review-packs.js --county kisumu` (do not edit by hand). 291 values. Procedure: [../../review-sop.md](../../review-sop.md). Kisumu is not yet on pages (lib/county-registry.js NOT_YET_ON_PAGES): nothing here is published, and data/reviews.json holds no record for it.

Each value lists what was stored, where it was read, the text as printed (or as read by OCR), the other sources for the same measure and period, and the result of the independent machine cross-check. The review-record template has `decision` and `reviewer_role` blank: an independent reviewer (never the extractor) fills them after opening the page, and adds the record to `data/reviews.json`. A machine cross-check is not review and may not be cited as the basis of a decision.

Machine check disagrees (check these first): `K060`, `K089`, `K090`, `K093`, `K094`.
